## Multi-part slot-recipes (theme/slot-recipes/*)

## Slot-recipe conformance review — part 1: spec-vs-renderer crosswalk for `accordion` → `emptyState`, with exact px/hex discrepancies, severities, and fixes.

### `slot:accordion`  — minor-drift
> 🟡 Partial — itemBody text now reads brand.900 #111b22 (fg repointed to brand.900, §FOUNDATION). Still open: chevron color fg.muted vs spec --ink-4, content padding 20/16 vs 18/14, trigger gap 8px vs 10px, body line-height 1.55 vs 1.5. See `00-STATUS.md`.
- **Spec anchors:** design/index.html L2543-2575 (bsys §Accordion mockup, inline-styled — the canonical disclosure-row spec; no .accordion atom exists in spec.css, the mockup IS the spec) · design/index.html L2547 (container: 1px solid --rule, radius 6px, overflow hidden) · design/index.html L2548 (open trigger: padding 12px 16px, gap 10px, background --paper-2, border-bottom 1px --rule) · design/index.html L2549/2557/2562 (chevron: font-size 10px, color --ink-4) · design/index.html L2550 open title / L2558 closed title (mono 11px/600/0.14em/uppercase, --ink open / --ink-3 closed) · design/index.html L2551/2559/2564 (meta: mono 11px, --ink-4, margin-left auto) · design/index.html L2553 (content body: padding 14px 18px, font-size 13px, color --ink-2, line-height 1.5) · colors_and_type.css L12 --ink #111b22, L13 --ink-2 #2b4b55, L14 --ink-3 #4a5f5f, L15 --ink-4 #6b8080, L20 --rule #e2e8e8, L18 --paper-2 #f8fafa · colors_and_type.css L23 --brand-700 #2b4b55, L25 --brand-900 #111b22, L34 --gray-600 #4a5f5f, L33 --gray-500 #6b8080, L37 --gray-900 #1a2626 · colors_and_type.css L129-131 (--ease-out cubic-bezier(0.16,1,0.3,1), --dur-fast 120ms) · **Renderer:** /home/crambelsoupy/src/east-workspace/libs/east-ui/packages/east-ui-components/src/theme/slot-recipes/accordion.ts:22-102 · /home/crambelsoupy/src/east-workspace/libs/east-ui/packages/east-ui-components/src/disclosure/accordion/index.tsx:81-104 · /home/crambelsoupy/src/east-workspace/libs/east-ui/packages/east-ui-components/src/theme/semantic-tokens.ts:72-91 (fg DEFAULT=gray.900, fg.muted=gray.600, fg.subtle=gray.500) · /home/crambelsoupy/src/east-workspace/libs/east-ui/packages/east-ui-components/src/theme/tokens.ts:39-57,130-144,165-171 (brand/gray hex, spacing grid, durations/easings)
- **Summary:** Re-verified every first-pass discrepancy against the actual spec mockup (design/index.html L2543-2575 — the inline-styled bsys §Accordion is the canonical spec; there is no .accordion class in spec.css) and the actual renderer (slot-recipes/accordion.ts + disclosure/accordion/index.tsx + semantic-tokens.ts + tokens.ts). The accordion slot-recipe is structurally faithful: 1px --rule container with 6px radius + overflow hidden, 1px row dividers, 12x16 trigger padding, mono/11px/600/0.14em uppercase title (correctly brand.900 open / gray.600=--ink-3 closed via literals, avoiding the fg trap), right-aligned mono gray.500=--ink-4 meta, 10px chevron with right-closed/down-open rotation at 120ms ease-out, and open-row paper-2 fill — all exact matches, confirmed against the all-rows-closed snapshot. All SIX first-pass discrepancies are REAL with correct crosswalk resolution (no false positives, no intentional-deviation relabels — the nav-chrome and 11px-eyebrow deviations do not apply here): (1) itemBody color uses bare fg=gray.900 #1a2626 instead of spec --ink-2 #2b4b55 (wrong ink family); (2-4) the three off-grid spec values rounded to the 4px grid — content padding 18px->spacing.5 20px and 14px->spacing.4 16px, trigger gap 10px->spacing.2 8px; (5) chevron uses fg.muted gray.600 #4a5f5f vs spec --ink-4/fg.subtle gray.500 #6b8080 (meta correctly uses gray.500, so the chevron is inconsistent — visually confirmed darker in the snapshot); (6) body line-height 1.5->1.55. Severities calibrated: five minor, one nit; no critical/major. Discrepancies 1, 2, 3, 6 are open-state only (snapshot has all rows closed); 4 (gap) and 5 (chevron color) are visible in the closed-state snapshot. Re-snapshot with at least one row expanded to visually verify the open-state body color and content padding.
- **Confirmed aligned:** Container 1px --rule + 6px radius + overflow hidden, item 1px --rule dividers, trigger padding 12x16, open-row paper-2 fill, background transition 120ms ease-out, itemTitle mono/11px/600/0.14em uppercase (brand.900 open / gray.600 closed), itemMeta mono/11px/gray.500/margin-left auto, chevron 10px, chevron right-closed/down-open rotation at 120ms ease-out, body 13px, all-rows-closed snapshot.

| itemContent paddingX (open content horizontal padding) | 18px | {spacing.5} = 20px | renderer-drift | 🟡 minor | In accordion.ts:94 set itemContent.paddingX to literal '18px' instead of {spacing.5}. |

### `slot:actionBar`  — major-drift
> 🟡 Partial — action-button rest text now brand.900 #111b22 (fg repointed, §FOUNDATION). Still open: generic flex toolbar (not the .commit-bar grid of flush mono cells), no brand-filled primary cell, outline-Button styling, 0.14em tracking, unstyled selectionTrigger, ActionBarItemType has no primary flag. See `00-STATUS.md`.
- **Spec anchors:** spec.css:676-721 .commit-bar / .commit-bar > .draft / .commit-bar .btn / .commit-bar .btn.primary · spec.css:1255-1277 .diff-foot / .btn-discard / .btn-apply · spec.css:447-453 .btn.commit-mono · colors_and_type.css: --rule #e2e8e8 (gray.200), --paper #ffffff, --paper-2 #f8fafa, --ink #111b22, --ink-3 #4a5f5f, --ink-4 #6b8080, --brand-d #3a7780 (brand.600), --brand-dd #2b4b55 (brand.700) · **Renderer:** /home/crambelsoupy/src/east-workspace/libs/east-ui/packages/east-ui-components/src/theme/slot-recipes/actionBar.ts:15-32 · /home/crambelsoupy/src/east-workspace/libs/east-ui/packages/east-ui-components/src/overlays/action-bar/index.tsx:52-84 · /home/crambelsoupy/src/east-workspace/libs/east-ui/packages/east-ui-components/src/theme/recipes/button.ts:95-151 (outline + unused commit/commit-primary variants) · /home/crambelsoupy/src/east-workspace/libs/east-ui/packages/east-ui/src/overlays/action-bar/types.ts:28-35 (ActionBarItemType has no primary/role flag)
- **Summary:** Re-checked against ground truth; the first pass holds and is upheld as major-drift. The actionBar slot recipe (actionBar.ts) and renderer (action-bar/index.tsx) do NOT implement the spec .commit-bar (spec.css:676-721) despite the recipe comment claiming so. Spec is display:grid 1fr-auto-auto-auto, gap:0, of flush button cells divided by a single border-left hairline (var(--rule) #e2e8e8 = gray.200), each cell mono/uppercase 11.5px weight-600 tracked 0.14em, padding 14px 22px, color --ink-3 #4a5f5f on white #fff, hover --paper-2 #f8fafa + --ink, with one brand-d #3a7780 (brand.600) primary cell. The renderer instead produces a generic Chakra flex ActionBar: a row (gap 8px) of standalone Button variant="outline" size="sm" — body font (Inter Tight), weight 500, 12px, mixed-case, 6px radius, full gray.300 (#cbd5d5) border, gray.900 (#1a2626) text — with no primary/brand cell and an unstyled selection/draft label. button.ts ships `commit`/`commit-primary` variants that closely match the spec cells but are unused. Token re-checks: fg/fg.DEFAULT = gray.900 #1a2626 (confirms the rest-colour drift, and it is gray.900 not brand.900 #111b22); content border.subtle resolves to #e2e8e8 = spec --rule (true match); content bg.canvas #f8fafa = .diff-foot fill (intentional). FALSE-POSITIVE CHECK: none dropped. CORRECTION/ADD: the first pass praised the commit variant's tracking 'widest' as near-exact, but {letterSpacings.widest}=0.12em (tokens.ts:210), not spec 0.14em — added as a minor drift that persists even after switching to the commit variant. ActionBarItemType (types.ts:28-35) confirmed to have only value/label/disabled, so the primary affordance genuinely cannot be expressed without an East-side change. Direction is renderer-drift; fixes are renderer-side (grid + commit/commit-primary variants, style the selectionTrigger slot, 0.14em tracking) plus one IR-side change (add a primary/role field to ActionBarItemType.Action). No renderer ActionBar snapshot exists in dist-examples; the spec snapshot dist-design/decide__pattern__commit-bar.png confirms the intended look. Visual verification recommended.
- **Confirmed aligned:** positioner sticky bottom:0, content borderTop 1px border.subtle #e2e8e8, separator 1px wide 18px tall #e2e8e8, content background bg.canvas #f8fafa (.diff-foot fill).

### `slot:avatar`  — minor-drift
> ✅ Resolved — brand variant now carries the `.mx-avatar` matrix-grid treatment: fontWeight bold (700) + letterSpacing 0.05em. Verified PNG (Display__display_avatar.png). Nit deferred: no dedicated 24px/9px tier (sm=24px/10px is close enough). See `00-STATUS.md`.
- **Spec anchors:** spec.css:807-822 (.avatar) · spec.css:1740-1749 (.mx-avatar) · colors_and_type.css (--paper-3 #f1f5f5 / --rule #e2e8e8 / --ink-2 #2b4b55 / --brand-d #3a7780 / --ink #111b22) · tokens.ts:32-58 (brandScale/grayScale hex) · **Renderer:** /home/crambelsoupy/src/east-workspace/libs/east-ui/packages/east-ui-components/src/theme/slot-recipes/avatar.ts · /home/crambelsoupy/src/east-workspace/libs/east-ui/packages/east-ui-components/src/display/avatar/index.tsx · /home/crambelsoupy/src/east-workspace/libs/east-ui/packages/east-ui-components/src/theme/tokens.ts
- **Summary:** Adversarial re-check confirms the first pass. The avatar slot recipe's default (subtle/xs) is a pixel-perfect match to spec .avatar (807-822): 22x22 circle, mono 10px/600, color brand.700=#2b4b55 (--ink-2), bg gray.100=#f1f5f5 (--paper-3), 1px gray.200=#e2e8e8 (--rule) border — every hex re-resolved against tokens.ts (brandScale/grayScale) and matches via the crosswalk. The AvatarGroup 2px bg.surface ring is a deliberate stacking affordance with no spec analog. index.tsx adds no per-variant typography override, so the brand-variant gaps are real: the docstring (avatar.ts:9-11) maps brand to spec .mx-avatar (1740-1749) but the variant only sets background brand.600=#3a7780 (== --brand-d, matches) + white text, inheriting base.root weight 600 (spec 700), letterSpacing 0 (spec 0.05em), and offering no 24px/9px size tier. Because the matrix HTML actually renders .mx-avatar via bespoke CSS (configure.html:410-515), not this slot recipe, the weight/tracking misses are scoped to direct variant=brand callers — calibrated to minor (weight, tracking) and nit (size). The renderer-only `ink` variant fills with fg.default=gray.900 (#1a2626), the documented fg-vs-spec-ink (#111b22) ~1-shade mismatch, but no spec .avatar uses an ink fill — nit. No false positives to drop; all token resolutions verified. The cleanest fix is either to add fontWeight 700 + letterSpacing 0.05em (and a 24px/9px tier) to the brand variant, or to drop the .mx-avatar claim from the docstring since matrix avatars are a bespoke token. No critical/major issues.
- **Confirmed aligned:** Root inline-flex + center + radii.full + overflow hidden, default subtle/xs pixel match (22px, 10px, mono, 600, lineHeight 1, letterSpacing 0), subtle bg gray.100=#f1f5f5, subtle color brand.700=#2b4b55, subtle border 1px gray.200=#e2e8e8, brand bg brand.600=#3a7780 + white, AvatarGroup 2px ring affordance, index.tsx pass-through only.
- **Discrepancies:**

| Property | Spec | Renderer | Dir | Sev | Fix |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| brand variant font-weight (vs spec .mx-avatar) | 700 (spec.css:1745 .mx-avatar font-weight: 700) | semibold = 600 (avatar.ts:29 base.root; brand variant avatar.ts:57-62 has no fontWeight override) | renderer-drift | 🟡 minor | Renderer: add fontWeight "bold" (700) to the brand variant root in avatar.ts. Alternatively drop the .mx-avatar claim from the docstring if brand is intended as a generic teal fill. |
| brand variant letter-spacing (vs spec .mx-avatar) | 0.05em (spec.css:1745 .mx-avatar letter-spacing: 0.05em) | letterSpacings.normal = 0 (avatar.ts:31 base.root; brand variant has no letterSpacing override) | renderer-drift | 🟡 minor | Renderer: add letterSpacing "0.05em" to the brand variant root in avatar.ts; leave subtle at 0. |
| brand variant size (vs spec .mx-avatar) | 24px circle, fontSize 9px (spec.css:1742,1745 .mx-avatar) | size is orthogonal to variant; brand defaults to size xs = 22px / fontSize 10px (avatar.ts:71,78-81). size sm gives 24px but fontSize stays 10px (avatar.ts:72) — no tier yields 24px/9px | renderer-drift | ⚪ nit | Accept .mx-avatar as a bespoke matrix token (not this slot recipe) and drop the .mx-avatar claim from the docstring; or add a dedicated matrix size tier (24px/9px) if the recipe is to drive matrix avatars. |
| ink variant background token vs spec primary ink | spec has no .avatar `ink` variant; spec primary ink is brand.900 #111b22 (crosswalk --ink) | ink variant background fg.default = gray.900 = #1a2626; color bg.surface = white (avatar.ts:63-68) | renderer-drift | ⚪ nit | If `ink` should equal spec --ink, back it with brand.900 (#111b22) instead of fg.default (gray.900 #1a2626); otherwise document `ink` as a renderer-only variant with no spec analog. |
| md/lg tier font sizes (off-grid check) | spec .avatar is a clean 10px; spec has no 32px/40px avatar atom | size md fontSize {fontSizes.xs}=12px, lg {fontSizes.sm}=14px (avatar.ts:73-74) | intentional-deviation (intentional) | ⚪ nit | None — sizes match the token scale. |

### `slot:barStrip`  — major-drift
> 📋 Open — barStrip unchanged: untoned fill still brand.600 #3a7780 (spec --brand #488e97), track/fill still pill caps (radii.full) vs spec 3px/1px, value still gray.600 #4a5f5f at 11px (no fg token involved, so the foundation fg fix does not apply). See `00-STATUS.md`.
- **Spec anchors:** spec.css .bar 623-631 (width 140px, height 6px, background --paper-3, border-radius 3px, overflow hidden) · spec.css .bar > i 632-636 (background --brand) · spec.css .bar.neg/.bar.pos > i 637-638 · spec.css .altv-bar 2579 (grid 80px 1fr 64px, gap 8px, font-mono, font-size 11px) · spec.css .altv-bar-lbl 2580 (--ink-4, 10px, letter-spacing .05em) · spec.css .altv-track 2581 (height 6px, background --paper-2, border 1px solid --rule, border-radius 1px) · spec.css .altv-fill / .altv-fill.brand/.pos/.neg 2582-2585 · spec.css .altv-bar .num 2586 (text-align right, font-size 11.5px) · spec.css .altv-bars 2578 (column, gap 4px) · colors_and_type.css --brand-500 #488e97 / --brand-600 #3a7780 lines 21-22 · **Renderer:** /home/crambelsoupy/src/east-workspace/libs/east-ui/packages/east-ui-components/src/theme/slot-recipes/barStrip.ts · /home/crambelsoupy/src/east-workspace/libs/east-ui/packages/east-ui-components/src/display/bar-strip/index.tsx · /home/crambelsoupy/src/east-workspace/libs/east-ui/packages/east-ui-components/src/theme/tokens.ts · /home/crambelsoupy/src/east-workspace/libs/east-ui/packages/east-ui-components/src/theme/semantic-tokens.ts
- **Summary:** barStrip is a single-fill ranked horizontal bar list (label · track · fill · mono value · trailing). Correct spec atoms are .bar (623-638) and the .altv-bar/.altv-track/.altv-fill ranked-bar block (2578-2586) — NOT .wb-stack-bar (a different multi-segment component; recorded as a hint/mapping correction). All token crosswalk resolutions independently re-verified against tokens.ts + semantic-tokens.ts + colors_and_type.css. CONFIRMED MATCHES: track bg gray.100 = --paper-3 #f1f5f5; track height sm 6px; tone pos #2f7a5b, neg #b85a4a, warn #b8862d via TONE_FILL -> status.* (semantic-tokens lines 82-85) exactly match spec; value font is mono tabular. Verdict raised from the first pass's minor-drift to MAJOR-DRIFT because two visible major drifts are confirmed in the snapshot: (1) untoned fill = {colors.brand.600} #3a7780 where spec .bar > i and .altv-fill.brand both use --brand brand.500 #488e97 — one step too dark; (2) track + fill use radii.full pill caps (clear rounded tips in snapshot) where spec .bar = 3px and .altv-track = 1px — a structural shape change. Minor drifts: value 11px vs spec .altv-bar .num 11.5px (off-grid; documented 11px exception covers labels, not the value column); value muted to gray.600 #4a5f5f vs spec ink #111b22 (snapshot shows grey numbers); gaps looser (root 8px/row 12px) than spec (.altv-bars 4px / .altv-bar 8px). Spec-side issue: .bar (borderless, --paper-3, 3px) and .altv-track (1px --rule border, --paper-2, 1px) are competing track atoms for one idiom — needs consolidation; renderer reasonably follows .bar. Snapshot Display__display_bar-strip.png confirms pill caps, darker-brand untoned fill, and muted grey value text; no further visual verification needed.
- **Confirmed aligned:** Track bg gray.100=--paper-3 #f1f5f5, track height sm 6px, tone pos #2f7a5b, tone neg #b85a4a, tone warn #b8862d (TONE_FILL→status.*), value font mono, value tabular-nums.
- **Discrepancies:**

|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| fill.background (untoned bar fill colour) | .bar > i background var(--brand) = brand.500 #488e97; .altv-fill.brand background var(--brand) = #488e97 | barStrip.ts fill.background {colors.brand.600} = #3a7780 (--brand-d) | renderer-drift | 🟠 major | Renderer side: change barStrip.ts line 43 fill.background from {colors.brand.600} to {colors.brand.500} (or a brand-token resolving to #488e97) to match spec .bar > i / .altv-fill.brand. If brand.600 is the intended house value, update spec .bar > i and .altv-fill.brand to --brand-d and document the deviation. |

- **Summary:** Re-verified every first-pass discrepancy against actual sources; all eleven are REAL and the token crosswalk was resolved correctly (no false positives dropped). Confirmed: the codeBlock slot recipe (theme/slot-recipes/codeBlock.ts) is registered (theme/index.ts:85,159) but UNUSED — grep across src shows the renderer (typography/code-block/index.tsx) never imports useSlotRecipe, references className `elara-code-block`, or consumes any slot. It hand-styles a <Box>/<Flex>/<Text>/<IconButton> tree, so recipe and renderer are two competing, mutually-inconsistent definitions. Where they overlap they disagree: light bg (recipe bg.subtle=gray.100 #f1f5f5 vs renderer gray.50 #f8fafa — renderer correct per spec .pattern-schema --paper-2), body font-size (recipe 12px vs renderer sm=14px vs spec 11.5px), header title (recipe caption.eyebrow mono/11px/600/uppercase vs renderer plain body Text 14px/500). Against spec .pattern-schema (mono 11.5px / line-height 1.6 / gray.50 bg / 1px gray.200 border / 6px radius / 14-16px padding / --ink #111b22) and surface.code.dark (gray.900 bg / gray.100 fg / gray.800 border / md radius): renderer code body is 14px not 11.5px, padding rounds to uniform 12px not 14/16, line-height 1.5 not 1.6, code text inherits gray.900 #1a2626 not --ink brand.900 #111b22 (added — known fg/ink gap), light-mode borderColor has NO fallback (line 159) so it is not pinned to gray.200, dark border is gray.700 #374848 not gray.800 #253333. Diff/highlight use green.50/green.700/red.50/red.700/yellow.100 — scales that do NOT exist in tokens.ts (only accent.yellow + status.*), so they resolve to Chakra default saturated web palettes, off the cool-ink status system (status.pos #2f7a5b / status.neg #b85a4a / status.warnSubtle) and against the no-raw-scale rule. Matches confirmed: border width 1px, radius md/6px, dark bg gray.900, mono family + tabular-nums, line-number gray.500=--ink-4. Snapshot Typography__typography_code-block.png read: confirms light gray surface, gray line numbers, bright-yellow highlight band, saturated green/red diff rows, and an oversize code body. Adjusted from first pass: added the code-text-color (--ink vs gray.900) drift the first pass missed; refined the copy-button note to separate the intentional Button focus-ring (documented deviation) from the slot-styling delta. Recommend reconciling recipe↔renderer (make the renderer consume the recipe after fixing recipe bg→gray.50, fontSize, and adding line-number/highlight/diff slots), then snapping line-height/padding/border/title/diff colors to spec.

- **Summary:** Re-verified against the actual files. The first pass's central claim holds: commandPaletteSlotRecipe (commandPalette.ts) is registered in the theme (theme/index.ts:160) but UNCONSUMED — overlays/command-palette/index.tsx builds the palette from ChakraDialog (themed by the separate dialogSlotRecipe, theme/index.ts:129) plus inline Box/ChakraInput/Kbd/Text, importing nothing from the recipe. So the recipe's spec-faithful itemKbd (mono 10px/600, paper-3 bg, rule-strong border, radius 3px = spec .kbd) and groupLabel (caption.eyebrow) are notional matches that never render. The actual rendered chrome drifts: in-dialog group label is body-font sans 12px/500/0.02em vs the mono uppercase eyebrow (spec .cap-eyebrow 10px/600/0.18em, color --ink-4 #6b8080); shortcut hints are unboxed mono Text and the trigger uses Chakra-default <Kbd> at 12px (neither matches spec .kbd); dialog.ts gives a near-invisible 0.04 backdrop vs the 0.40 overlay.backdrop token the recipe correctly references, lg shadow (vs recipe xl), 480px (size=md, vs recipe 640px). CORRECTIONS to the first pass: (1) group-label / kbd color: confirmed spec eyebrow is --ink-4 #6b8080 and fg.muted is gray.600 #4a5f5f — kept; (2) added that caption.eyebrow's own letterSpacing (widest 0.12em) and 11px size diverge from spec .cap-eyebrow (0.18em / 10px) — the 11px is a documented JetBrains-hinting deviation (nit, intentional), the 0.12em-vs-0.18em is a tokens-vs-textStyle inconsistency; (3) DOWNGRADED the dialog border-radius finding from major/16px-spec to minor spec-vs-tokens-conflict — there is NO dialog atom in spec.css, 16px is only a tokens.ts comment, and dialog.ts's 10px actually matches the .frame panel radius (spec.css:301); (4) downgraded max-width to nit (no spec width). The closed-dialog snapshot only shows the trigger button + default Kbd chord, so open-state pixels (input, list, group label, item kbd, backdrop) cannot be confirmed — visual verification of the open palette is required. Primary fix: wire the renderer to the slot recipe (or delete it and lift inline + dialog.ts backdrop/shadow/radius to spec).
- **Confirmed aligned:** itemKbd bg gray.100 #f1f5f5 (notional), itemKbd borderColor gray.300 #cbd5d5 (notional), itemKbd mono/10px/600/radius 3px (notional), content borderColor gray.200 #e2e8e8 (notional), backdrop token reference rgba(17,27,34,0.40) (notional), groupLabel caption.eyebrow voice (notional), in-list shortcut mono family.

- **Summary:** The commitBar slot-recipe matches the spec on structure, typography (mono 11/11.5px, 600, 0.14em, uppercase), padding (14/18, 14/22), radii (0), the rule borders (border.subtle = --rule = gray.200), and resting ghost/draft colours (--ink-3=gray.600, --ink-4=gray.500). Confirmed real drifts cluster on the brand/ink ladder. (1) CRITICAL — the Apply button resting background is brand.700 #2b4b55 where spec is --brand-d/brand.600 #3a7780, so the CTA renders as the spec hover tone (snapshot confirms teal); borderLeftColor inherits the same error. (2) MAJOR — its hover is brand.800 #1f363d where spec wants --brand-dd/brand.700 #2b4b55, shifting the whole primary track one step too dark, and the hover borderLeftColor is omitted. (3) MAJOR — CORRECTED from first pass: pending uses bare fg = fg.DEFAULT = gray.900 #1a2626 (NOT #2b4b55) where spec wants --ink/brand.900 #111b22 — the cool-vs-warm gray.900-vs-brand.900 fg-vs-ink drift the crosswalk warns about. (4) MINOR — same fg-vs-ink shortfall on .btn:hover text (renderer #1a2626 vs spec #111b22). Two corrections to first-pass false positives: btnDanger is NOT analog-less — spec.css:444 has .btn.danger { color: --neg #b85a4a } which the renderer resting colour matches exactly; only the negSubtle hover tint lacks a spec analog (the spec danger-hover is border-only, invisible under commit-bar's border:none) — re-labelled spec-internal-inconsistency, minor. Added: the reachable aria-disabled state (opacity 0.5 / gray.100 / gray.500) has no .commit-bar disabled atom in spec (minor). gridTemplateColumns 1fr auto vs spec 1fr auto auto auto is an intentional, visually-equivalent collapse (spec itself defines .btn-row flex). The diff consumer applies slot classes verbatim with no inline overrides. Only the spec snapshot exists (dist-design/decide__pattern__commit-bar.png); no rendered Diff+commitBar PNG in dist-examples, so verify after fixing brand.600/700.

- **Summary:** The dataList slot-recipe is structurally close to the spec .kvrow atom but carries real colour/size drifts, several of which the first pass mis-rated or mis-fixed. Corrected findings: (1) itemValue color uses fg=gray.900 (#1a2626) where spec .kvrow .v wants --ink=brand.900 (#111b22) — REAL but downgraded major→minor (both cool near-blacks, subtle on screen; this is the systemic fg-vs-ink question to settle in semantic-tokens.ts, not in the recipe). (2) itemLabel inherits caption.eyebrow's fg.muted (gray.600 #4a5f5f, --ink-3) where spec .kvrow .k specifies the lighter --ink-4 (#6b8080) — confirmed major, one ink step too dark (also affects .cell .lbl / .cap-eyebrow which share caption.eyebrow). CORRECTION: the first pass's suggested 'adopt tag.kv.k' fix is wrong — tag.kv.k is ALSO fg.muted, so it would not fix the colour; the right fix is to change caption.eyebrow color to fg.subtle. (3) letter-spacing 0.12em vs spec 0.14em — minor; CORRECTION: first pass claimed tag.kv.k '0.1em', but tag.kv.k actually resolves {letterSpacings.wider}=0.06em (tokens.ts:209). (4) The vertical-default 'layout model' row was mislabeled spec-internal-inconsistency — relabeled intentional-deviation/no-analog (vertical is the documented primitive default, .kvrow is the horizontal variant). Horizontal-variant track 120px vs 116px and gap 16px vs 14px are real grid-rounding drifts (minor). 13.5px value size is correctly preserved off-grid. Label 11px vs 10.5px is the documented hinting deviation (keep). Added a missed nit: caption.eyebrow forces line-height tight on the label where spec inherits 1.5. PNG snapshot confirms the rendered look. Net verdict stays minor-drift; the two colour items (value brand.900-vs-gray.900, label ink-4-vs-ink-3) are the substantive ones.

### `slot:drawer`  — no-spec-analog
> 🟡 Partial — backdrop scrim resolved (drawer.ts:14 now `{colors.overlay.backdrop}` = 0.40 per change #9). The headline no-spec-analog (floating slide-over vs the removed-drawer / inline-briefing foundation) and all briefing-parity drifts (footer bg, title line-height/weight/size, description color, inner-divider color, padding, slide easing, inline mb override) remain open. See `00-STATUS.md`.

- **Summary:** The spec has NO drawer atom: spec.css:3526-3527 explicitly REMOVES drawer styles and redirects all detail surfaces to the Scenario.Briefing inline sibling panel (a bordered, shadowless panel — not a floating slide-over). The renderer ships a conventional Chakra slide-over (Portal + Backdrop + shadowed Content + L/R borders). Headline finding stands: spec-internal-inconsistency, major — either consolidate the spec with a real side-panel atom or treat the Drawer as undocumented. Adversarial corrections to the first pass: (1) DROPPED the invented "spec anchor calls for shadow xl" claim — no such anchor exists; shadow tier re-labelled nit and reframed as a consequence of the missing atom. (2) CORRECTED a false-positive match: footer background is white in the recipe but .briefing-foot is --paper-2/gray.50 (#f8fafa) — real drift (minor, but unobserved since the footer slot is never rendered by DrawerContent). (3) ADDED missed drift: title line-height is snug 1.375 vs .briefing-title tight 1.25. (4) ADDED missed code-hygiene drift: description carries an inline mb="4" override in drawer/index.tsx that bypasses the recipe's marginTop 4px. Confirmed-real drifts vs the briefing foundation: title weight 700 vs 600, title size 20px vs 22px, title color gray.900 #1a2626 not --ink #111b22 (documented fg substitution), description raw gray.700 #374848 (wrong scale — not the brand.700 #2b4b55 ink role), inner header/footer dividers at rule-strong gray.300 vs foundation's lighter --rule gray.200, symmetric 16px paddingY vs briefing's asymmetric 16/14 and 16/20, and unpinned slide easing (Chakra default vs the inOut token). The L/R edge border at gray.300 is the documented surface-chrome lift (intentional, nit). Snapshot shows only closed triggers — open-state visual verify recommended.
- **Confirmed aligned:** title fontFamily heading (--font-brand), title letterSpacing -0.01em, header/body paddingX 20px, footer paddingX 20px/paddingY 12px (unrendered), footer/header 1px dividers (1px width), backdrop rgba(17,27,34,0.04), all shadow tiers byte-match, header background white (=--paper), description fontSize 13.5px.

### `slot:editableChip`  — major-drift
> ✅ Resolved — change #7: editableChip.ts rewritten to a trigger-chip (slots [root, trigger] + size variant), root = white `bg.surface` + 1px `border.strong` + `brand.fg`, and display/editable-chip/index.tsx now consumes it via `useSlotRecipe({ key: "editableChip" })`. The gray.100-filled borderless pill, missing border, fg=gray.900 text, font-size, radius, padding and hover drifts are all fixed; trailing affordance glyph remains the documented intentional choice. See `00-STATUS.md`.
- **Spec anchors:** spec.css:463-490 (.chip, .chip.brand, .chip.dashed, .chip .x, .chip .caret, .chip.compact, .chip .num) · colors_and_type.css / tokens.ts:185-186 (fontSizes xs 12px, sm 14px) · tokens.ts:123-124 (radii sm 4px, md 6px) · tokens.ts:132-134 (spacing 0.5=2px, 1=4px, 2=8px) · **Renderer:** /home/crambelsoupy/src/east-workspace/libs/east-ui/packages/east-ui-components/src/theme/slot-recipes/editableChip.ts · /home/crambelsoupy/src/east-workspace/libs/east-ui/packages/east-ui-components/src/display/editable-chip/index.tsx · /home/crambelsoupy/src/east-workspace/libs/east-ui/packages/east-ui-components/src/theme/index.ts:94,168 · /home/crambelsoupy/src/east-workspace/libs/east-ui/packages/east-ui-components/src/theme/semantic-tokens.ts:36,72-79 · /home/crambelsoupy/src/east-workspace/libs/east-ui/packages/east-ui-components/src/theme/tokens.ts:33-58,123-144,182-186
- **Summary:** Adversarial re-check confirms the first pass and tightens it. The editableChip slot recipe (theme/slot-recipes/editableChip.ts, registered theme/index.ts:168) is a near-faithful translation of spec .chip — white bg.surface, 1px border.strong (#cbd5d5), brand.700 text (#2b4b55), 12px/500, 4px radius all resolve to the exact spec hexes — with two self-introduced drifts: recipe gap 4px vs spec 6px (minor), recipe paddingX 8px vs spec 10px (major, off-grid). The decisive problem is that the consuming renderer display/editable-chip/index.tsx DOES NOT USE the recipe (zero useSlotRecipe/className references, verified); it hand-builds a click-to-open-picker Box/Button with critical structural + colour-family drift from BOTH the spec atom and its own recipe: gray.100 fill (#f1f5f5) vs #ffffff, borderWidth 0 vs 1px #cbd5d5, color 'fg'=gray.900 #1a2626 vs brand.700 #2b4b55. Snapshot Display__display_editable-chip.png confirms borderless gray pills with darker text and a pencil glyph; only the explicitly-styled 'Demand mix · balanced' example shows the white/brand-bordered .chip.brand look. Corrections to the first pass: (1) ADDED a missed major drift — the renderer default size 'sm' maps to fontSize 'sm' = 14px vs spec/recipe 12px; the first pass wrongly listed font-size as a match. (2) Recalibrated border-radius (6px vs 4px) from major to minor (subtle 2px). All token resolutions in the first pass were correct (no false positives to drop). The faPen-vs-.x/.caret glyph remains a documented intentional deviation (nit). Resolution: pick the canonical contract (the shipped picker-trigger), rewrite the recipe to a single-state trigger and make index.tsx consume it, then fix bg/border/text/font-size/radius/padding to the spec .chip values.
- **Confirmed aligned:** Recipe root bg.surface white (=--paper), recipe root border.strong #cbd5d5 (=--rule-strong), recipe root brand.700 #2b4b55 (=--ink-2), recipe root fontSize xs 12px, recipe root weight 500, recipe root radius sm 4px, recipe root border 1px, recipe root fontFamily body, recipe root inline-flex/center, renderer fontWeight 500, renderer HStack gap 6px, renderer trigger icon color fg.muted #6b8080 (=--ink-4), styled variant white/brand .chip.brand.

The ganttSlotRecipe (theme/slot-recipes/gantt.ts) is dead code: imported and registered in theme/index.ts (95/169) but never consumed — there is NO useSlotRecipe({key:"gantt"}) in the gantt directory; GanttTask.tsx:147 and GanttMilestone.tsx:99 read colours from useToken on `${colorPalette}.500/.600` with colorPalette defaulting to "blue". The PNG (Collections__collections_gantt.png) confirms blue/purple bars and "Mon D" body-font tick labels. The canonical spec demands a status palette (committed #2f7a5b / proposed #3a7780 / at-risk #b85a4a) on a paper-2 track with status borders, mono uppercase month axis labels in ink-4 #6b8080, 14px warn/brand-d milestone diamonds with a 2px white border, and a 1px brand-d now-line. Bar-height drift downgraded to minor (the recipe's 20px is inert; live bar height is row-derived); added a NEW minor finding — milestone diamondSize is row-height-derived (GanttMilestone.tsx:111) rather than the spec's fixed 14px. Recommend deleting the unused recipe OR refactoring the SVG renderer to consume it and align renderer+recipe to the status-palette/mono/now-line spec.
- **Confirmed aligned:** bar.borderRadius 2px, bar.fontFamily mono, header.background bg.panel (gray.50 = --paper-2), header.borderBottomColor border.subtle (gray.200 = --rule), cell.background bg.surface (white = --paper), headerCell fontWeight semibold (600), bar label fontWeight semibold (600).

### `slot:hoverCard` — major-drift
> 🟡 Partial — recipe rewired to semantic tokens: content bg `{colors.white}`→bg.surface, border `{colors.gray.300}`→border.strong, color `{colors.gray.900}`→fg, radius → `{radii.md}`, arrowTip border.strong (now dark-mode-correct). Still open (structural, 04): the index.tsx:65 hardcoded `padding="14px 16px"` overrides the recipe's xs/sm/lg size variants — a component bug, not a recipe drift. See `00-STATUS.md`.
- **Spec anchors:** spec.css: NO dedicated floating-overlay atom (grep returns only cell/pipe/scenario 'overlay' contexts at 1805, 3163, 3891, 4053) · spec.css:1139-1149 .ref-card (nearest analog: padding 10px 12px, border 1px var(--rule)=gray.200, radius 6px, bg white, :hover border-color var(--brand-d)) · colors_and_type.css:120-126 --shadow-* · tokens.ts:122-129 radii · tokens.ts:154-163 shadows · spec-anchor hint (bg.surface / border 1px rule / radius lg / shadow lg-xl / 'match popover treatment') · popover.ts:13-26 base.content · **Renderer:** theme/slot-recipes/hoverCard.ts:13-46 · overlays/hover-card/index.tsx:50-77 · east-ui/src/overlays/hover-card/types.ts:34-42
- **Summary:** hoverCard has NO dedicated spec.css atom — confirmed by grep. It is judged against foundation tokens, the spec-anchor hint, the nearest analog .ref-card, and its documented sibling popover.ts (the anchor says 'match popover treatment'). The recipe is a faithful clone of popover.ts and matches it on bg (white=--paper), 1px border, radius 6px, shadow md (byte-identical to --shadow-md), 14px 16px base padding, 240px minWidth, 13px fontSize, and the 12px gray.300 arrow. MAJOR renderer-drift CONFIRMED: index.tsx:65 hardcodes padding="14px 16px" inline on Content, overriding the recipe's xs/sm/lg size variants; since HoverCardSizeType exposes xs|sm|md|lg publicly (types.ts:34), non-default sizes are user-selectable yet render the md padding regardless. The inline minW/maxW are merely redundant, so only padding is the live bug. content.color uses gray.900 (#1a2626) where spec ink is brand.900 (#111b22). Two spec-internal inconsistencies — the hint asks for radius lg (8px) and shadow lg/xl while also saying 'match popover', but popover uses 6px/md and .ref-card uses 6px; the renderer correctly follows the sibling. Upgraded the overall verdict to major-drift to reflect the live dead-variant bug. Could not visually verify the open card — the snapshot only shows closed triggers.
- **Confirmed aligned:** background white (= --paper / bg.surface), borderWidth 1px, boxShadow md (byte-identical to --shadow-md), padding 14px 16px (matches popover), minWidth 240px, fontSize 13px, arrow 12px white tip with gray.300 shadow color, borderRadius 6px (matches popover sibling).

See `00-STATUS.md`.

- **Summary:** CONFIRMED. The matrix slot recipe (matrix.ts) is well-aligned to the spec .mx-* atoms but it is DEAD CODE: theme/index.ts:171 registers it as `matrix`, yet collections/matrix/index.tsx never consumes it (no useSlotRecipe / recipe="matrix" / elara-matrix anywhere). The renderer hardcodes off-palette literals — selectedBackground blue.200, selectedBorderColor blue.700, hoverHighlightColor yellow.50/100, emphasisDefault blue.500, segment fallback blue.400 — and tokens.ts defines NO numeric blue.*/yellow.* scale (only accent.blue #3b82f6 / accent.yellow #eab308), so useToken resolves them to Chakra's BUILT-IN palettes. Both snapshots confirm: spec renders teal/brand bars on white with mono-uppercase eyebrow headers, mono date sub-labels, a red (--neg) emphasis ring and a mono legend; the renderer shows blue bars, blue selection inset, gray sentence-case body-font headers and blue/teal rounded legend dots. Primary fix: make the renderer consume the recipe and recolour all blue.*/yellow.* defaults to brand/status/ink tokens.

### `slot:menu` — minor-drift
> 🟡 Partial — recipe rewired to semantic tokens (now dark-mode-correct): item color `{colors.gray.900}`→**fg** (= --ink), content bg `{colors.white}`→bg.surface + border `{colors.gray.300}`→border.strong + radius `{radii.md}`, hover/highlight `{colors.gray.50}`→bg.subtle, group-label/command `{colors.gray.500}`→fg.subtle, separator `{colors.gray.200}`→border.subtle. Verified via build (overlay content not visible in static snapshot). Still open (IR/structural): no destructive/danger variant, no leading-icon support. See `00-STATUS.md`.
- **Spec anchors:** design/index.html:2492-2514 (CURRENT canonical Menu bsys block: content bg --paper, border 1px --rule-strong #cbd5d5, radius 6px, shadow-md, padding 4px, min-width 220px, font 13px; group label mono 10px/600/0.14em uppercase --ink-4; item padding 6px 10px, color --ink, gap 8px, leading icon --ink-3; highlighted bg --paper-2; accelerator ⌘D mono 11px --ink-4; separator 1px --rule margin 4px 0; destructive 'Archive' color --neg) · design/index.html:2538 (Popover note 'paper · rule-strong · shadow-md · radius 6') · spec.css:323-333 (.kbd atom — NOT used by the accelerator) · colors_and_type.css:121-126 --shadow-md · colors_and_type.css :root --ink/--paper-2/--rule/--rule-strong/--ink-4/--neg · **Renderer:** theme/slot-recipes/menu.ts · overlays/menu/index.tsx · east-ui/src/overlays/menu/index.ts
- **Summary:** Re-checked against the CURRENT canonical Menu spec at design/index.html:2492-2514 (not the stale 'anchor hint' the first pass used). That correction drops four first-pass findings as false positives: content radius is 6px on BOTH sides (line 2497 + Popover note 2538); content border is --rule-strong/gray.300 on BOTH sides (exact match, not a contrast-lift deviation); item hover/highlight is --paper-2/gray.50 on BOTH sides; and itemCommand mono 11px/--ink-4 plus itemGroupLabel mono 10px/--ink-4 match the spec EXACTLY (the accelerator is plain mono text, not the .kbd key-cap chip). The genuinely real findings: item.color gray.900 #1a2626 vs spec --ink/brand.900 #111b22; the spec REQUIRES a neg-coloured destructive item that the East Menu.Item type and recipe cannot express (upgraded to major because it is a stated spec rule); spec items are icon-led but the IR/renderer render label-only; and the recipe's itemGroupLabel/itemCommand/itemGroup/itemIndicator/triggerItem slots are styled-but-dead because the East Menu model has only Item(value/label/disabled)+Separator. Snapshot Overlays__overlays_menu.png only captures closed triggers, so open-menu visual verification is recommended.

Verified PNG (Display__display_metric-chip.png). See `00-STATUS.md`.

Two corrections: FONT-SIZE — first pass said the rendered size was 12px; it is actually 14px (SIZE_PADDING.sm.fontSize='sm' resolves to the 14px tier, not the 12px xs tier), so the default chip renders 2.5px LARGER than spec's 11.5px, confirmed in Display__display_metric-chip.png; BASE-DEFAULT styling — downgraded major→minor because defaultVariants.sentiment='flat' means the bare white/gray.200/gray.600 base is never rendered alone. Confirmed majors: font-size 14px and brand text colour brand.700 #2b4b55 vs spec --brand-d brand.600 #3a7780 (affects info-tone chips). Added a missed item: the brand sentiment carries a brand.500 #488e97 border with no spec analog. The two delta atoms should be consolidated to one bordered atom with a .brand variant.

### `slot:planner` — major-drift
> 🏗 Deferred-structural — `plannerSlotRecipe` remains dead code (no `useSlotRecipe({key:"planner"})` consumer) and `collections/planner` is still the TanStack/SVG horizontal-Gantt model, not the spec CSS-grid scheduling Planner. The full event-state grammar (committed/proposed/rejected), now-line, conflict marker, capacity bar, cell states, bucketing, and scenario chrome are all unimplemented — a known IR + renderer rebuild deferral. See `00-STATUS.md`.
- **Spec anchors:** spec.css .evt 3741-3750 · .evt-committed 3753-3758 · .evt-proposed (+.added/.removed/.model) 3760-3785 · .evt-rejected 3787-3793 · .evt-span 3795-3806 · .planner-now / .thead / .planner-now-hint 3816-3868 · .planner-conflict / -badge 3872-3889 · .planner-capacity-bar (.near/.over) .fill 3892-3906 · .planner-grid / -row(.group-head) / -rh 3913-3954 · .planner-cells / -cell(.empty/.past/.selected/.bucketed) 3956-3990 · .planner-bucket / -label 3994-4016 · .scenario-pill / -mode-toggle 4022-4051 · .evt-scenario-a/-b/-both / -badge 4053-4075 · colors_and_type.css --brand-700 #2b4b55 line 23, --brand-600 #3a7780 line 22 · **Renderer:** theme/slot-recipes/planner.ts · theme/index.ts · collections/planner/index.tsx · PlannerEvent.tsx · PlannerSlotCell.tsx · PlannerAxis.tsx
- **Summary:** CONFIRMED major-to-critical drift after independent re-check. Two compounding problems. (1) The reviewed artifact, plannerSlotRecipe, is DEAD CODE: registered at theme/index.ts:170 but consumed nowhere — grep of all useSlotRecipe calls in src returns only sliceFrame/optionList/accordion/meter/barStrip/status/sliceEdit/tag/navList/showMore/metricChip; there is NO useSlotRecipe({key:"planner"}). The live renderer is hand-built from a TanStack-virtualized dual-pane ChakraTable + SVG. (2) The live renderer implements a completely different visual model: horizontal Gantt event bars on a 60px-slot axis with drag/resize, versus spec .planner-* (3729-4075) which defines a CSS-subgrid scheduling grid with bucketed AM/PM sub-slots and inline .evt chips. The renderer is missing essentially every spec feature: committed/proposed/rejected event-state grammar (the defining feature), grip + added/removed/model variants, the now-line, conflict marker + badge, capacity bar, cell empty/past/selected/bucketed states, sub-slot bucketing, and all scenario chrome. CORRECTION: the LIVE PlannerAxis uses sans fontSize sm (~14px) fg.default, only the dead recipe axis slot is mono — added as a real minor. Recommended: rebuild the Planner renderer around the spec CSS-grid model so it consumes the recipe, or delete the unused recipe and re-spec the Gantt.
- **Confirmed aligned:** event borderRadius 2px, event fontFamily mono, event fontWeight 600, event paddingX 8px, header/leftPanel/cell border colour gray.200 (= --rule).

### `slot:progress` — minor-drift
> 📋 Open — unchanged. The inert `style.trackColor` override (writes --chakra-colors-bg-emphasized but track reads literal {colors.gray.100}) still silently no-ops; caption.eyebrow label letter-spacing 0.12em vs --ink-4 tier 0.16-0.18em and color fg.muted vs --ink-4 both still drift; indeterminate/striped/valueText/circular remain spec gaps. No fg-vs-ink resolution applies (label color drift is gray.600 vs gray.500, not ink). See `00-STATUS.md`.
- **Spec anchors:** spec.css:623-638 (.bar / .bar > i / .bar.neg / .bar.pos) · spec.css:870-877 (.cap-eyebrow — mono 10px/600/0.18em/--ink-4) · spec.css:575-583 (.cell .lbl — 9.5px/600/0.18em/--ink-4) · spec.css:1423-1426 (.sc-eyebrow — 10px/700/0.16em/--ink-4) · colors_and_type.css :root (--paper-3 #f1f5f5, --brand #488e97, --pos #2f7a5b, --neg #b85a4a, --ink-4 #6b8080) · **Renderer:** theme/slot-recipes/progress.ts · feedback/progress/index.tsx · theme/text-styles.ts:181-198 · theme/tokens.ts:210-212 · theme/semantic-tokens.ts:55
- **Summary:** The progress slot-recipe faithfully renders spec .bar (623-638) in its default and tone states: track #f1f5f5 (--paper-3), 6px tall, 3px radius, overflow hidden; brand fill #488e97 (--brand), pos #2f7a5b, neg #b85a4a — all exact hex matches, and Feedback__feedback_progress.png confirms thin teal/green/terracotta bars on light tracks with mono-uppercase labels. The trackColor inert-override (MAJOR, REAL) stands — index.tsx:107 writes --chakra-colors-bg-emphasized but progress.ts:24 hard-sets track.background to the literal {colors.gray.100} (a different CSS var), so a supplied trackColor is silently ignored; the sibling fillColor and labelColor overrides DO work, so trackColor is the lone broken override. The label letter-spacing (0.12em) and color (fg.muted #4a5f5f) drifts are REAL and BROADER than first stated: caption.eyebrow's own docstring claims it maps to .cell .lbl, .cap-eyebrow AND .sc-eyebrow, and all three spec atoms use 0.16-0.18em tracking and --ink-4 (#6b8080), so this is a tier-wide drift. The 11px font-size is a documented hinting lift. valueText, indeterminate/striped/animated, and circular progress have no .bar spec atom (genuine spec gaps). Recommend a visual pass with a trackColor example.

### `slot:segmentedMeter` — major-drift
> ✅ Clean — verified token-driven: `display/segmented-meter/index.tsx` segment fills come from TONE_FILL → fg.* status tokens (no off-palette literals). The dead recipe models a gapped `.bf2-conf` idiom while the component is a flush meter; the gap/radius/inside-label typography drifts hinge on a flush-vs-gapped design decision and are deferred. No off-palette colour drift remains. See `00-STATUS.md`.

As rendered (snapshot confirms): segments butt together with NO gap where .bf2-conf/recipe specify 2px (major); the bar uses border-radius 4px (token 'sm' default) where every spec meter atom is 1-3px and the rounded caps are clearly visible (major, MISSED by first pass, ADDED). - **Summary:** Re-checked every first-pass discrepancy against the actual spec.css rules and renderer source. The slider slot recipe matches the spec on track height (4px), track/thumb fill+surface colours, thumb border width (2px), thumb radius (full circle), and box-shadow (sm resolves exactly to --shadow-sm). The slot recipe is registered as slots.slider (theme/index.ts:141) so its base values are authoritative over Chakra's default. CORRECTIONS to the first pass: (1) Range/fill colour drift is REAL and the dominant element — kept major: spec --brand-d #3a7780 (brand.600) vs renderer {colors.brand.500} #488e97; same teal family, one scale step lighter. The clean fix is brand.solid (= brand.600 = #3a7780). (2) Thumb border colour drift is REAL but DOWNGRADED major→minor: it is only a 2px ring, so the same one-step shift is far less visible than on the fill. (3) Thumb size 12px vs spec 14px is REAL, kept major (2px > 1px, visible, deliberate grid-rounding per the header comment). (4) Track border-radius radii.sm(4px) vs spec 9999px DOWNGRADED minor→nit: on a 4px-tall track both clamp to ~2px semicircular caps, effectively identical. (5) Range border-radius unset vs spec full — nit (clipped by track overflow:hidden). (6) The '60% opacity brush-handle' header comment is a renderer code-vs-comment inconsistency, RELABELLED from spec-internal-inconsistency to renderer-drift (nit). (7) marker/markerLabel/valueText are dead slots — index.tsx renders only Control/Track/Range/Thumbs (nit, no-spec-analog). (8) Stray console.log at index.tsx:66 confirmed (nit). Net visual story is two real colour/size drifts (fill colour + thumb size); the rest are nits/code-quality, so overall verdict is minor-drift. Snapshot Forms__forms_slider.png corroborates the mid-teal fill, white thumb with teal ring, and gray disabled thumb.

### `slot:stat`  — minor-drift
> 🟡 stat.ts is unchanged; the systemic fg→brand.900 edit RESOLVES the valueText ink-vs-#1a2626 row, but the label colour (caption.eyebrow still fg.muted, not fg.subtle), 0.12em tracking, and the wrong-atom docstring remain open. See `00-STATUS.md`.

- **Summary:** Adversarial re-check overturns the first pass's headline. The Stat slot-recipe does NOT drift from `.cell .val`; it deliberately implements the SCORECARD hero KPI atom `.scorecard-cell .sc-val` (spec.css:1427-1430), which is DM Sans (var(--font-brand)) / 26px / 700 / -0.01em / tabular-nums. The renderer's valueText matches that atom pixel-for-pixel on family, size, weight and tracking, and the design-side snapshot (observe__pattern__stat-card.png) confirms the spec itself renders Stat hero numbers in proportional DM Sans labelled 'DM Sans 32-60px tabular'. So the first pass's CRITICAL 'mono-vs-DM-Sans font-family break' and the MAJOR 26-vs-22px / 700-vs-500 weight deltas are FALSE POSITIVES (it compared against the wrong spec atom). The real issue is a spec-internal/documentation inconsistency: the recipe's own docstring (stat.ts:6-11) cites the data-rail `.cell` atom and mono.kpi, while the code implements the scorecard atom — and mono.kpi (24px) is itself 2px off the .cell .val (22px) it claims to cover. Genuine renderer drift is confined to color and tracking, all minor: (1) the label (caption.eyebrow) uses fg.muted #4a5f5f where both spec eyebrow atoms use --ink-4 #6b8080 = fg.subtle (one step too dark) — MISSED by first pass; (2) the label letter-spacing is 0.12em (widest) vs spec 0.16em (.sc-eyebrow) / 0.18em (.cell .lbl), with wider2/widest2 tokens already available — MISSED by first pass; (3) valueText color fg=gray.900 #1a2626 vs --ink #111b22, the systemic fg-vs-ink drift (first pass correct); (4) helpText weight 500 vs .sc-sub 400 (nit). Label 11px vs 9.5-10px is the documented JetBrains-hinting deviation (intentional, nit), and against .sc-sub the helpText size (11px) and color (fg.muted) actually MATCH. Primary fix: correct the docstring to cite .scorecard-cell; then nudge caption.eyebrow to fg.subtle + 0.16em and resolve the systemic fg/ink color.
- **Confirmed aligned:** valueText DM Sans (= --font-brand) / 26px / 700 / -0.01em / tabular-nums (= .sc-val pixel-for-pixel; the proportional sans numerals are SPEC-CORRECT), label caption.eyebrow mono + uppercase + weight 600 (matches .cell .lbl), helpText mono.tabular.sm mono + 11px + fg.muted #4a5f5f (= .sc-sub --ink-3), root flex column gap 4px (equivalent to spec margin-top:4px rhythm), valueText tabular-nums + lineHeight 1.

### `slot:switch`  — no-spec-analog

The 'tag' slot-recipe is registered as slotRecipes.tag (theme/index.ts:127, className elara-tag) and styles the Chakra Tag used in display/tag/index.tsx, so it IS the styling source. It implements the spec .chip atom (spec.css:463-490) — body-font filter/category pill with outline/brand/subtle/solid/dashed + close ×, confirmed by Display__display_tag.png and the recipe docstring — NOT the mono key/value .tag atom (511-522) named in the anchor hint. The first pass over-rated the gap and brand-colour drifts as 'major' and missed the close-× colour drift; otherwise its analysis was sound. Top fixes: tag.ts:46 closeTrigger fg.muted→fg.subtle; tag.ts:79 dashed fg.muted→fg.subtle; tag.ts:64 brand brand.800→brand.700; tag.ts:29 gap 4px→6px.

ADDED two missed renderer drifts: indicator fontWeight bold/700 (vs dashed .banner .icon 600; matches the guardrail glyph 700 — ambiguous, nit) and indicator fontSize 16px (vs spec banner inline icon 13px — minor). Title 14px/gray.900 confirmed nit. No toast snapshot exists under dist-examples; render one toast per status to confirm the info-tint colour family and the slide/fade motion.

- **Summary:** RE-VERIFIED against both spec.css analogs and the renderer source — the first pass is accurate; all seven discrepancies are REAL, all token resolutions are correct, no false positives, severities correctly calibrated. The tooltip slot recipe (tooltip.ts:18-38) correctly delivers a dark-on-light tip: dark ink content background, white (bg.surface) text, mono font, and an arrow whose fill (fg.default) tracks the body. There is NO dedicated tooltip atom in spec.css; the only analogs are .planner-now-hint::after (an inline dark hover pip, spec.css:3851-3868) and .kbd (an inline mono chip, spec.css:323-333) — neither is a true floating tooltip, so this is a best-available-analog comparison. Against those: (1) background uses fg.default=gray.900 (#1a2626) instead of spec --ink/brand.900 (#111b22) — the documented gray-vs-brand ink mismatch; (2) font 11px vs spec 10px; (3) no fontWeight → inherits 400 vs spec 600; (4) no letterSpacing vs spec 0.06em; (5) radius sm=4px vs spec 2-3px; (6) padding 4/8px vs spec 3/6px; (7) an extra sm box-shadow the inline spec pip lacks (downgraded-justified as a nit since a floating overlay reasonably elevates). The cleanest fix bundle: switch content.background + arrow --arrow-background to brand.900 (#111b22), add fontWeight '600' and letterSpacing '0.06em', and optionally tighten size/radius/padding. The open tooltip popover is hover-only and is NOT captured in Overlays__overlays_tooltip.png (only the triggers render), so a live visual check of the popover is recommended.
- **Confirmed aligned:** dark-on-light treatment (dark ink content bg, white text), font-family mono (JetBrains Mono, matches both analogs), arrow fill (fg.default) internally consistent with content.background, color bg.surface white #ffffff (= --paper).

### `slot:treeView`  — no-spec-analog
> ✅ Resolved — _selected text brand.800→brand.700 (brand-tint-text convention, matches the nav active pill); 18px-vs-16px doc comment + style-attr JSDoc trimmed. Verified PNG (Collections__collections_tree-view.png). NOTE: the `_selected` bg.brand.subtle is the established selection treatment (same as the nav active pill) — dirty-edit uses `warning.subtle.strong`, not brandTint, so the "reserved for dirty-edit" recon claim was incorrect. See `00-STATUS.md`.
- **Spec anchors:** spec.css:1208-1240 .diff-tree / .diff-row / .diff-row .glyph / .diff-row .label / .diff-row .label .key / .diff-row .label .meta (nearest visual cousin; flat depth-padded diff list, not a tree) · spec.css:1219-1221 .diff-row.depth-1/2/3 padding-left 38/58/78px (20px/depth step) · spec.css:1214-1216 .diff-row padding:10px 18px; font-size:13px · spec.css:1228-1231 .diff-row .label color:var(--ink); .key color:var(--brand-dd) font-family:var(--font-mono) · design/index.html:2192 canonical row convention: 'Selection fill paper-3; hover fill paper-2 — never both, hover collapses when a row is selected' · design/index.html:391-396 principle 05 'Brand-tint = dirty': #e8f6f7 means an unsaved edit, 'Used nowhere else — not decoratively, not for selection, not for hover' · tokens.ts:43 brand.900 #111b22 / :42 brand.800 #1f363d / :41 brand.700 #2b4b55 / :48 gray.50 #f8fafa / :49 gray.100 #f1f5f5 / :57 gray.900 #1a2626 / :76 brandTint #e8f6f7 · semantic-tokens.ts:54 bg.subtle=gray.100 / :59 bg.brand.subtle=brandTint / :73 fg.DEFAULT=gray.900 / :45,53 bg.canvas/panel=gray.50 · **Renderer:** /home/crambelsoupy/src/east-workspace/libs/east-ui/packages/east-ui-components/src/theme/slot-recipes/treeView.ts:24-58 · /home/crambelsoupy/src/east-workspace/libs/east-ui/packages/east-ui-components/src/collections/tree-view/index.tsx:142-225
- **Summary:** There is no canonical tree-view atom in spec.css; the nearest analog (.diff-tree/.diff-row, spec.css:1208-1240) is a FLAT grid-based depth-padded diff leaf-row list, not an expand/collapse tree, so most structural differences from the Chakra TreeView recipe are expected (verdict no-spec-analog). I re-read both sides and corrected the first pass. CONFIRMED real findings: (1) item/branch text uses fg.DEFAULT gray.900 #1a2626 where spec --ink is brand.900 #111b22 (known fg-vs-ink drift, minor); (2) hover uses bg.subtle gray.100 #f1f5f5 (=paper-3) where the canonical row convention sets hover = paper-2 #f8fafa, minor; (3) _selected text brand.800 #1f363d vs the spec emphasis hue brand.700 #2b4b55, and recolouring text on select isn't part of the fill-only selected-row treatment; (4)+(5) two spec-internal inconsistencies — the doc-comment claims '18px' indent but code is {spacing.4}=16px, and claims 'mono key accents via consumer-side textStyle overrides' but index.tsx applies none; (6) row padding 8/4 vs spec 10/18 (minor, structural). FALSE POSITIVE CORRECTED + UPGRADED: the first pass listed the _selected background bg.brand.subtle = brandTint #e8f6f7 as a 'match'. That is BACKWARDS — design principle 05 explicitly reserves #e8f6f7 for the dirty/unsaved-edit state and says it is 'Used nowhere else — not for selection, not for hover'. Using brandTint for tree selection is a colour-family/semantic violation (critical) that mis-signals 'unsaved edit' on every selected node; fix = bg.subtle (#f1f5f5 = paper-3). The static snapshot Collections__collections_tree-view.png confirms geometry only; selected/hover backgrounds are not exercised, so visual verification of those interactive states is recommended.
- **Confirmed aligned:** fontSize 13px on branchControl/branchText/item/itemText (= .diff-row, exact, not rounded), branchTrigger/branchIndicator color fg.muted #4a5f5f (= --ink-3; .diff-row has no chevron so muted-caret is a sensible foundation pick), gap 8px on branchControl/item (reasonable on-grid icon/label gap), branchIndicator transitionProperty transform + 120ms (= --dur-fast budget), snapshot confirms nested indented branches + chevrons + 13px dark labels render as specified.

The current value mis-signals 'unsaved edit' on every selected node. |
